-- Food Truck Permits: city health department permits for food trucks.
--
-- Roles: operator (truck owners), inspector (field inspections and the live
-- board), supervisor (environmental health supervisor: suspends permits and
-- approves operators with a past suspension, recording the reason).
-- Role rows are provisioned by the local operator (service:operator), the
-- same pattern as `guide staging`: signup alone grants no role.
use identity password

table roster:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('operator', 'inspector', 'supervisor')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  online only

mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('operator', 'inspector', 'supervisor')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roster[principal] { principal, role }

query myRole():
  return roster[viewer]

invariant roster.read only .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
invariant roster.insert only viewer = 'service:operator'

-- Trucks belong to exactly one operator. activePermit points at the truck's
-- current permit once one is issued (maintained by approveApplication).
table trucks:
  owner: principal
  name: text 1..80
  licensePlate: text 1..16
  activePermit: permits ?
  unique licensePlate else PLATE_TAKEN
  immutable owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation registerTruck(name: text 1..80, licensePlate: text 1..16):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  return insert trucks { owner: viewer, name, licensePlate }

query myTrucks(c: cursor ?):
  return trucks where .owner = viewer first 50 by .id after c

query allTrucks(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return trucks first 50 by .id after c

invariant trucks.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant trucks.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']

-- Each application must include interior photos, the fire extinguisher tag
-- photo and the permit fee: all three columns are required, so an
-- application row cannot exist without them.
table applications:
  truck: trucks
  owner: principal
  interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4
  extinguisherTag: image <=5000000
  feeCents: int 1..100000000
  status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'inspection recorded', 'approved', 'failed') default 'applied'
  failedAt: time default now
  lastPassed: bool default false
  createdAt: time default now
  immutable truck, owner, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents, createdAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation submitApplication(truck: trucks, interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4, extinguisherTag: image <=5000000, feeCents: int 1..100000000):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  t = trucks[truck] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require t.owner = viewer else NOT_YOUR_TRUCK
  flag = operatorFlags[viewer]
  require flag = null or flag.suspended = false else OPERATOR_SUSPENDED
  return insert applications { truck, owner: viewer, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents }

query myApplications(c: cursor ?):
  return applications where .owner = viewer first 50 by .createdAt after c

-- The live board: every application as it moves applied -> inspection recorded ->
-- approved / failed. Staff only.
query board(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return applications first 50 by .createdAt after c

invariant applications.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant applications.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']

-- v2 renamed the 'inspected' stage to 'inspection recorded'. The column still
-- admits the old value so that every historical row survives adoption and
-- rollback without dormancy; the migrateInspectionRecorded mutation below
-- moves every remaining 'inspected' row to the renamed stage (run after
-- deploy and after every forward), and no workflow mutation writes the old
-- value anymore. Rollback to a release without the renamed value projects
-- 'inspection recorded' back to 'inspected' instead of hiding rows.
down applications inspectedView(status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'inspection recorded', 'approved', 'failed')): {status: 'inspected' if status = 'inspection recorded' else status}

-- One-shot data migration for the v2 rename. Supervisor-only. Moves a single
-- remaining 'inspected' application to 'inspection recorded'; callers page
-- the board until none remain.
mutation migrateInspectionRecorded(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspected' else NOT_INSPECTED
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded' }
  return applications[app.id]

-- Inspections are written by inspectors (or supervisors). owner is the truck
-- owner's principal, denormalized so operators read their own reports and
-- staff read everything.
table inspections:
  truck: trucks
  application: applications ?
  permit: permits ?
  inspector: principal
  passed: bool
  notes: text <=2000 ?
  kind: enum('initial', 'reinspection', 'routine')
  inspectedAt: time default now
  owner: principal
  immutable truck, application, permit, inspector, passed, notes, kind, inspectedAt, owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

-- First inspection of an application: applied -> inspection recorded.
mutation inspectApplication(application: applications, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'applied' else NOT_APPLIED
  i = insert inspections { truck: app.truck, application: app.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'initial', owner: app.owner }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded', lastPassed: passed }
  return i

-- Reinspection of a failed application, only within ten days of the failure.
mutation reinspectApplication(application: applications, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'failed' else NOT_FAILED
  require now <= app.failedAt + 10d else REINSPECTION_LATE
  i = insert inspections { truck: app.truck, application: app.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'reinspection', owner: app.owner }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded', lastPassed: passed }
  return i

-- Decision on an inspection-recorded application. Approval issues the permit, valid
-- 365 days; rejection marks it failed and starts the ten-day reinspection
-- window.
mutation approveApplication(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspection recorded' else NOT_INSPECTED
  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS
  p = insert permits { truck: app.truck, owner: app.owner, application: app.id, issuedAt: now, expiresAt: now + 365d, status: 'active', flagged: false, failedAt: now }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'approved' }
  update trucks[app.truck] { activePermit: p.id }
  return p

mutation rejectApplication(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspection recorded' else NOT_INSPECTED
  require not app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'failed', failedAt: now }
  return applications[app.id]

query myInspections(c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .owner = viewer first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

query applicationInspections(application: applications, c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .application = application first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

query permitInspections(permit: permits, c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .permit = permit first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

invariant inspections.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant inspections.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']

-- Permits are issued on approval and live 365 days. A failed routine
-- inspection flags the permit; a passing reinspection within ten days
-- clears the flag, otherwise the daily sweep suspends it.
table permits:
  truck: trucks
  owner: principal
  application: applications
  issuedAt: time default now
  expiresAt: time
  failedAt: time default now
  flagged: bool default false
  status: enum('active', 'suspended', 'expired') default 'active'
  immutable truck, owner, application, issuedAt, expiresAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

-- Routine inspection (or reinspection) of an active permit.
mutation inspectPermit(permit: permits, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  p = permits[permit] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.status = 'active' else PERMIT_NOT_ACTIVE
  require now < p.expiresAt else PERMIT_EXPIRED
  i = insert inspections { truck: p.truck, permit: p.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'reinspection' if p.flagged else 'routine', owner: p.owner }
  update permits[p.id] { flagged: not passed, failedAt: now }
  return i

query myPermits(c: cursor ?):
  return permits where .owner = viewer first 50 by .issuedAt after c

query allPermits(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return permits first 50 by .issuedAt after c

invariant permits.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']

-- Suspensions are written by the supervisor (recording the reason) or by
-- the daily sweep when the ten-day reinspection window lapses (reason
-- recorded, suspendedBy null for the automatic case).
table suspensions:
  permit: permits
  owner: principal
  reason: text 1..500
  suspendedBy: principal ?
  suspendedAt: time default now
  immutable permit, owner, reason, suspendedBy, suspendedAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation suspendPermit(permit: permits, reason: text 1..500):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  p = permits[permit] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.status = 'active' else PERMIT_NOT_ACTIVE
  require now < p.expiresAt else PERMIT_EXPIRED
  update permits[p.id] { status: 'suspended', flagged: false }
  upsert operatorFlags[p.owner] { operator: p.owner, suspended: true }
  return insert suspensions { permit: p.id, owner: p.owner, reason, suspendedBy: viewer }

query mySuspensions(c: cursor ?):
  return suspensions where .owner = viewer first 50 by .suspendedAt after c

invariant suspensions.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']

-- An operator whose permit was suspended cannot apply again until the
-- supervisor approves them, recording the reason.
table operatorFlags:
  operator: principal
  suspended: bool default true
  unique operator
  immutable operator
  read <- .operator = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

table approvals:
  operator: principal
  reason: text 1..500
  approvedBy: principal
  approvedAt: time default now
  immutable operator, reason, approvedBy, approvedAt
  read <- .operator = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation approveOperator(operator: principal, reason: text 1..500):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  flag = operatorFlags[operator] ! else OPERATOR_UNKNOWN
  require flag.suspended else OPERATOR_NOT_SUSPENDED
  update operatorFlags[operator] { suspended: false }
  return insert approvals { operator, reason, approvedBy: viewer }

query myFlag():
  return operatorFlags[viewer]

query myApprovals(c: cursor ?):
  return approvals where .operator = viewer first 50 by .approvedAt after c

query flaggedOperators(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  return operatorFlags where .suspended = true first 50 by .id after c

-- Daily sweep: suspend active flagged permits whose reinspection window
-- lapsed, and expire permits past their 365-day life.
job sweepPermits(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):
  for p in permits:
    if p.status = 'active' and p.flagged and p.failedAt < periodEnd - 10d:
      update permits[p.id] { status: 'suspended', flagged: false }
      insert suspensions { permit: p.id, owner: p.owner, reason: 'No passing reinspection within ten days of the failed inspection.', suspendedAt: periodEnd }
      upsert operatorFlags[p.owner] { operator: p.owner, suspended: true }
    if p.status = 'active' and p.expiresAt <= periodEnd:
      update permits[p.id] { status: 'expired', flagged: false }

schedule dailySweep every 1d at 03:00: sweepPermits
-- Mobile Notary Signings.
-- Roles: owner (agency owner), dispatcher, notary, title_company.
-- A signing always carries the signer's ID scan and the signing fee.
-- Notaries read operational fields but never the fee or billing notes;
-- only dispatcher/owner read those. Title companies read only their own
-- signings. Status flows requested -> assigned -> signed -> returned, and
-- returned must land within 24h of signing. Only the owner approves a
-- notary for out-of-state papers.
use identity password
use personas alice, bob

-- Roster: exactly one role per principal. Only the local operator
-- (staging/production bootstrap) writes it; app code never self-assigns.
table roles:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('owner', 'dispatcher', 'notary', 'title_company')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  cap 1000
  online only

mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('owner', 'dispatcher', 'notary', 'title_company')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roles[principal] { principal, role }

query myRole():
  return roles[viewer]

-- Out-of-state approval per notary. Notaries register themselves; only the
-- owner flips the approval flag.
table notaryProfiles:
  principal: principal
  approvedOutOfState: bool default false
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
  insert <- .principal = viewer
  update <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']
  cap 1000
  online only

mutation registerNotary():
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'notary'] else NOT_NOTARY
  if exists notaryProfiles[principal = viewer]:
    return notaryProfiles[principal = viewer]
  return insert notaryProfiles { principal: viewer }

mutation approveNotary(notary: principal, approved: bool):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER 'Only the agency owner approves notaries for out-of-state papers.'
  require exists roles[principal = notary, role = 'notary'] else NOT_A_NOTARY 'That principal is not a notary.'
  p = notaryProfiles[principal = notary] ! else NOT_REGISTERED 'That notary has not registered.'
  update notaryProfiles[p.id] { approvedOutOfState: approved }
  return null

query notaryDirectory():
  return roles where .role = 'notary' first 100 by .principal

query approvalDirectory():
  return notaryProfiles first 100 by .principal

table signings:
  titleCompany: principal
  notary: principal ?
  signerName: text 1..120
  address: text 1..300
  documents: [text <=200] 1..20
  idScan: image <=10000000
  fee: money USD
  agencyBillingNotes: text <=2000 ? renamed from billingNotes
  outOfState: bool default false
  status: enum('requested', 'assigned', 'signed', 'returned') default 'requested'
  signedAt: time ?
  returnedAt: time ?
  createdAt: time default now
  immutable titleCompany, createdAt
  read <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'notary'] or .titleCompany = viewer
  read (fee, agencyBillingNotes) <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
  insert <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'title_company'] and .titleCompany = viewer
  update <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or .notary = viewer
  cap 5000
  online only

-- Title companies file signing requests for themselves. The ID scan and the
-- fee are required: omitting either refuses at decode time.
mutation createSigning(signerName: text 1..120, address: text 1..300, documents: [text <=200] 1..20, idScan: image <=10000000, fee: money USD, agencyBillingNotes: text <=2000 ? alias billingNotes, outOfState: bool ?):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'title_company'] else NOT_TITLE_COMPANY
  return insert signings { titleCompany: viewer, signerName, address, documents, idScan, fee, agencyBillingNotes, outOfState: outOfState ?? false }

-- Dispatchers and the owner assign a registered notary. Out-of-state papers
-- need an owner-approved notary.
mutation assignSigning(signing: signings, notary: principal):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF 'Only dispatch staff assign signings.'
  require exists roles[principal = notary, role = 'notary'] else NOT_A_NOTARY 'That principal is not a notary.'
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'requested' else BAD_STATUS 'Only requested signings can be assigned.'
  if s.outOfState:
    p = notaryProfiles[principal = notary] ! else NOT_APPROVED 'That notary is not approved for out-of-state papers.'
    require p.approvedOutOfState else NOT_APPROVED 'That notary is not approved for out-of-state papers.'
  update signings[signing] { notary, status: 'assigned' }
  return null

-- The assigned notary (or dispatch staff) records the signing.
mutation markSigned(signing: signings):
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'assigned' else BAD_STATUS 'Only assigned signings can be marked signed.'
  require s.notary = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_ASSIGNED 'Only the assigned notary or dispatch staff records the signing.'
  update signings[signing] { status: 'signed', signedAt: now }
  return null

-- Signed papers ship back within one day of signing; later returns refuse.
mutation markReturned(signing: signings):
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'signed' else BAD_STATUS 'Only signed signings can be marked returned.'
  require s.signedAt != null else BAD_STATUS 'This signing has no signing time.'
  require now <= s.signedAt + 24h else RETURN_OVERDUE 'Signed papers must ship back within one day of signing.'
  require s.notary = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_ASSIGNED 'Only the assigned notary or dispatch staff records the return.'
  update signings[signing] { status: 'returned', returnedAt: now }
  return null

-- Dispatch staff adjust the fee charged to the title company. Only the
-- agency owner may change the agency billing notes; the dispatcher may
-- still read them but cannot change them.
mutation updateBilling(signing: signings, fee: money USD, agencyBillingNotes: text <=2000 ? alias billingNotes):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF 'Only dispatch staff edits billing.'
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  if agencyBillingNotes != s.agencyBillingNotes:
    require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER_NOTES 'Only the agency owner changes agency billing notes.'
  update signings[signing] { fee, agencyBillingNotes }
  return null

-- Live dispatch board: every caller sees exactly the rows (and columns)
-- their rules admit, newest arrivals last.
query board(c: cursor ?):
  return signings first 100 by .id after c

query signingDetail(signing: signings):
  return signings[signing]

-- Privacy intent, stated independently from the rules above.
invariant signings.read only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'notary'] or .titleCompany = viewer
invariant signings.fee read only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
invariant signings.agencyBillingNotes read only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
invariant signings.updateBilling only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
invariant signings.insert only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'title_company']
invariant notaryProfiles.approveNotary only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']
invariant notaryProfiles.approvedOutOfState update only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']
invariant signings.assignSigning only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/security.md:387:### N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY
snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/security.md:389:N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY: An argument-supplied column has a specific change guard on edit that creation does not imply. The question names that column, editor and guard, rather than a table-wide permission. Confirm creation authority against the specification; enforce the change guard at creation when required and test independent column write intent with non-null input. Exact changed-field comparisons in a stable single-write editor are supported. Target keys must be fixed inputs/IDs, and the editor cannot change its authority dependencies. Mixed conditions, changed branch aliases, mutable selectors, multiple target writes and early-return equivalents remain outside this heuristic, with generic insert inventory retained when applicable. Absence of advice is not a safety proof.

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/security.md:393:N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY: A caller can initialize a private column without its change guard and outside its column read audience. This question ranks above ordinary asymmetry because the caller supplies private agency data it cannot read. Confirm who may supply this field at creation; enforce the guard when required and test independent column write intent with a non-null value. Read privacy alone does not define write authority; the question is advisory.

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/security.md:397:N_EVIDENCE_REMOVAL: Delete, detachment or explicit clearing can remove parent-linked media evidence. Ordinary non-null photo replacement is not removal; optional attachments without required creation linkage or independent integrity intent are excluded. This question is advisory.

//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let args=&p.args;
    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
            }
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    }
    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            let valid=match (k,literal){
                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
            };
            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
        }
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
        }}
        cases=next;
    }
    let focused=super::executed::write_arguments(bound,p).into_iter().filter(|a|matches!(p.args.get(a),Some(ir::ArgType::Optional(_)))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if focused.is_empty(){return cases;}
    let mut prioritized=Vec::new();
    for case in cases{
        for name in &focused{if let Some(value)=arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.clone(),p.args[name].clone())]),4).and_then(|mut a|a.remove(name))&&value!=Value::Null{
            let mut alternate=case.clone();alternate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !prioritized.contains(&alternate){prioritized.push(alternate);}
        }}
        if !prioritized.contains(&case){prioritized.push(case.clone());}
        if prioritized.len()>=LIMIT{prioritized.truncate(LIMIT);out.unsupported.insert("column-write argument frontier capped at 512; remaining combinations uncovered".into());break;}
    }prioritized
}
// Preserve status/decision alternatives on each resource tuple before the
// small directed cap; text/noise arguments must not correlate bools with ids.
fn directed_cases(p:&Program,cases:Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let identities=p.args.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Ref{..}|ir::ArgType::Principal)).map(|(n,_)|n).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let original=cases.clone();
    let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    for base in cases{let key=serde_json::to_string(&identities.iter().map(|n|(*n,base.get(*n))).collect::<Vec<_>>()).unwrap();if !seen.insert(key){continue;}
        let mut group=vec![base.clone()];
        for (name,kind) in &p.args{let values=match kind{ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().cloned().map(Value::String).collect(),_=>Vec::new()};
            for value in values{let mut candidate=base.clone();candidate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !group.contains(&candidate){group.push(candidate);}}
        }groups.push(group);
    }
    let mut out=Vec::new();for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &groups{if let Some(args)=group.get(n){out.push(args.clone());}}}
    if original.iter().any(|case|!out.contains(case)){campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed argument grouping omits correlated/non-status alternatives; remaining combinations uncovered",p.name));}
    out
}
pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
pub(super) fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
}
pub(super) fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    typed_disclosure_image(ty,data,table,key,column,physical,None)
}
pub(super) fn typed_disclosure_image(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value,policy:Option<&RuleExpr>)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)))&&policy.is_none_or(|p|!p.any_term(&|term|{
        let column=match term{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::OldField(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>Some(c.as_str()),RuleTerm::Reference{column,..}|RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,..}=>Some(column.as_str()),_=>None};
        column.filter(|c|*c!="$security:server-clock").is_some_and(|c|data.field(c).is_err()||data.field(c).ok()!=physical.field(c).ok())
    }));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
        Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)=>typed_disclosure_image(inner,data,table,key,column,physical,policy),
        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure_image(inner,r,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure_image(ty,v,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
        _=>false,
    }
}
pub(super) fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(bound,&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
    fn statement_inner(&mut self, statement: &S, out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
        match statement {
            S::As { principal, body, site } => self.as_viewer(principal, body, site, out),
            S::Return { value, site } => {
                if self.viewer_depth == Some(self.depth) { return Err(job_reads::diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_FLOW", "return cannot leave an as block", "assign a local inside as, then return outside it")); }
                let prior = std::mem::replace(&mut self.returned_data, self.depth == 0 && self.kind == ProgramKind::Query);
                let result = self.value_of(value, out);
                self.returned_data = prior;
                let (expr, ty) = result?;
                self.returns.push((ty, value.clone()));
                out.push(Stmt::Return(expr));
                Ok(())
            }
            S::Bind { names, value, site } => {
                if names.len() == 2 {
                    return self.tuple_bind(names, value, site, out);
                }
                if names.len() != 1 {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "tuple bindings take exactly two names and sorted(a, b) or divmod(a, b)", "(quotient, remainder) = divmod(dividend, divisor)"));
                }
                let name = &names[0];
                if self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.parameter) {
                    return Err(unknown(site, format!("{name} is an argument; locals cannot shadow arguments"), format!("rename the local (for example {name}2)")));
                }
                if let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(name).cloned()
                    && let Ty::List(item, max) = &binding.ty
                    && let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value
                    && op == "+" && matches!(left.as_ref(), A::Name { name: source, .. } if source == name)
                    && let A::Array { items, .. } = right.as_ref()
                    && items.len() == 1
                {
                    let (element, ty) = self.value_of(&items[0], out)?;
                    let mut origins = binding.origins.clone();
                    origins.push((Ty::List(Box::new(ty.clone()), 1), value.clone()));
                    let joined = if *max == 0 { ty.clone() } else { item.join_item(&ty).map_err(|error| self.assignment_type_error(self.join_error_many(error.at("[]"), &origins), name, value, site))? };
                    if !ty.assignable_to(&joined) || !item.assignable_to(&joined) {
                        return Err(self.assignment_type_error(type_error(site, format!("{name} holds {}; cannot append {}", item.name(), ty.name()), "append an item of the list's type"), name, value, site));
                    }
                    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.1 — append in place; the heap meter is the accumulated list's bound.
                    binding.ty = Ty::List(Box::new(joined), u32::MAX);
                    binding.origins = origins;
                    binding.declared = binding.ty.clone();
                    binding.fields.clear();
                    binding.error = None;
                    binding.revision += 1;
                    out.push(Stmt::Push { target: binding.runtime.clone(), value: element });
                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.clone(), binding);
                    return Ok(());
                }
                let (expr, ty) = self.value_of(value, out)?;
                if matches!(value, A::Write { op: WriteOp::Delete, .. }) {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "delete does not yield a value; return a confirmation value after the write", "delete the row, then `return null`"));
                }
                let count_capture = self.count_capture(name, value);
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.1 — rebinding preserves the local slot and resets its flow refinement.
                if let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(name).cloned() {
                    let mut origins = binding.origins.clone();
                    origins.push((ty.clone(), value.clone()));
                    let joined = binding.declared.join(&ty).map_err(|error| self.assignment_type_error(self.assignment_join_error(error, name, &origins), name, value, site))?;
                    if ty != Ty::Null && binding.declared != Ty::Null && !ty.value_compatible(&binding.declared) {
                        return Err(self.assignment_type_error(type_error(site, format!("{name} is {}; cannot rebind it to {}", binding.declared.name(), ty.name()), "use a different local for a different type"), name, value, site));
                    }
                    binding.declared = if self.loops > 0 { joined.rebound() } else { joined };
                    binding.ty = if self.loops > 0 { ty.rebound() } else { ty };
                    binding.origins = origins;
                    binding.fields.clear();
                    binding.error = None;
                    binding.revision += 1;
                    out.push(Stmt::Assign { name: binding.runtime.clone(), value: expr });
                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.clone(), binding);
                } else {
                    let runtime = self.declare(name, ty.clone());
                    self.maintenance_local(&runtime, site, name);
                    self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().get_mut(name).unwrap().origins.push((ty, value.clone()));
                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime, value: expr });
                }
                if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.loops + self.caller_loops == 0
                    && matches!(domain, A::If { condition, .. } if super::super::intent_variants::condition(condition).is_some()) {
                    let mut leaf = domain;
                    while let A::If { then, .. } = leaf { leaf = then; }
                    let table = match leaf { A::Read(read) => read.table.as_str(), A::Merge(merge) => merge.table.as_str(), _ => "table" };
                    return Err(placement(site, "argument-presence alternatives cannot be a job sweep domain",
                        format!("for {name} in {table}:  -- put the optional filter and work inside this stable sweep")).guide("jobs"));
                }
                let (loop_domain, item_ty) = self.loop_domain(domain, out)?;
                if self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.parameter) {
                    return Err(unknown(site, format!("{name} is an argument; locals cannot shadow arguments"), "give the loop item a fresh name"));
                }
                let before = self.visible_bindings();
                let (binding, body_out) = self.loop_body(name, item_ty, domain, body, site, matches!(loop_domain, LoopDomain::Page(_)) && !self.source_may_write(body))?;
                if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.loops + self.caller_loops == 0 && matches!(&loop_domain, LoopDomain::Page(scan) if scan.take == Bound::Meter) {
                    let after = self.visible_bindings();
                    if let Some((name, _)) = before.iter().find(|(name, old)| !name.starts_with('$') && after.get(*name).is_some_and(|new| old.revision != new.revision)) {
                        return Err(placement(site, format!("automatic sweep carries local {name} across iterations, but chunks commit independently"), format!("declare {name} inside the `for row in table:` body for per-item work, or store cross-chunk progress in a table")).guide("jobs"));
                    }
                }
                self.sources.insert(format!("for/{binding}"), (site.clone(), self.catalog.source.expression(domain)));
                out.push_for(binding, loop_domain, body_out);
                Ok(())
            }
            S::Break { site } => {
                if self.loops == 0 {
                    if self.viewer_depth.is_some() { return Err(job_reads::diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_FLOW", "break cannot leave an as block or helper", "put break in a loop inside this block or helper")); }
                    return Err(placement(site, "break ends the enclosing for or chunk, and there is none here", "delete this line; a program ends with return"));
                }
                let bindings = self.binding_states();
                self.loop_transfers.last_mut().expect("an enclosing loop").breaks.push(bindings);
//! Expanded helpers can write from any expression block.
// @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — legacy eligibility examines the complete expanded write footprint.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn stmts_write_tables(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Insert { table, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "insert")),
            Stmt::Update { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "update")),
            Stmt::Upsert { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "upsert")),
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, upsert, .. } => out.push((target.table.clone(), if *upsert { "upsert" } else { "update" })),
            Stmt::Delete { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "delete")),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => out.push((target.table.clone(), "delete")),
            _ => {}
        }
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_write_tables(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_write_tables(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_write_tables(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                stmts_write_tables(then_body, out);
                stmts_write_tables(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_write_tables(start, out); expr_write_tables(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_write_tables(&recurse.seed, out);
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_write_tables(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_write_tables(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                for key in &current.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_write_tables(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_write_tables(&condition.setup, out); expr_write_tables(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_write_tables(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_write_tables(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_write_tables(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
        match key { IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) | IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(expr) => expr_write_tables(expr, out) }
    }
    for cursor in [&scan.after, &scan.before].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(cursor, out); }
}

fn expr_write_tables(expr: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(_) => {}
        Expr::Field { base, .. } => expr_write_tables(base, out),
        Expr::Array(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr_write_tables(base, out); fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)); }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            expr_write_tables(left, out);
            expr_write_tables(right, out);
        }
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
        Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Get(read) | Expr::Exists(read) => {
            read.key.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out));
        }
        Expr::Block { body, value } => { stmts_write_tables(body, out); expr_write_tables(value, out); }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            if let Some(after) = merge.position.as_ref().and_then(|position| position.after.as_ref()) { expr_write_tables(after, out); }
            for lane in &merge.lanes {
                if let Some(guard) = &lane.guard { expr_write_tables(guard, out); }
                if let Some(project) = &lane.project { expr_write_tables(project, out); }
                if let Some(value) = &lane.distinct { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                stmts_write_tables(&lane.setup, out);
                if let Some(source) = &lane.source {
                    if let Some(scan) = source.scan() { scan_write_tables(scan, out); }
                    for value in source.expressions() { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                }
                scan_write_tables(&lane.scan, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

//! The catalog: every table (declared or generated for a maintain) with its
//! columns and indexes, plus the named shapes and functions, and where each
//! schema path was declared so a core validation issue can point at a line.

use crate::ast::*;
use crate::diag::{Diagnostic, Site, name_rewrite, unknown};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct TableInfo {
    pub through: Option<snapback4_core::schema::through::Through>,
    pub captures: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,
    pub recorded: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,
    pub decl: TableDecl,
    pub columns: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,
    pub indexes: Vec<IndexDecl>,
    /// Set when the table is a maintain's generated target.
    pub maintained: Option<MaintainDecl>,
}

impl TableInfo {
    pub fn total_unique(&self, index: &IndexDecl) -> bool {
        index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.iter().all(|column| column == "id" || self.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — a scan cursor carries declared components only;
    // uniqueness with a nullable component cannot resolve ties between nulls.
    pub fn total_cursor(&self, index: &IndexDecl) -> bool {
        index.columns.iter().any(|column| column == "id") || index.unique && index.words.is_none()
            && index.columns.iter().all(|column| match index.computed.get(column) {
                Some(expr) => super::time_indexes::key(&self.decl.name, &self.columns, column, expr).is_ok(),
                None => self.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional),
            })
    }
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Catalog {
    pub ordinary_searches: Vec<String>,
    pub source: crate::source::SourceMap,
    pub feed_views: BTreeMap<String, super::feed_view::Plan>,
    pub extremum_domains: BTreeMap<String, ReadExpr>,
    pub read_origins: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub canonical_read_origins: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub view_reads: BTreeMap<String, crate::ast::MergeExpr>,
    pub view_types: BTreeMap<String, crate::types::Ty>,
    pub planner_notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    /// Exact cap declarations that would admit an otherwise fully verified
    /// owner-or-membership page, keyed by the read's source site.
    pub membership_cap_repairs: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub synthesized: std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
    pub documents: Vec<Document>,
    pub tables: BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>,
    pub maintains: BTreeMap<String, MaintainDecl>,
    pub shapes: BTreeMap<String, ShapeDecl>,
    pub functions: BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl>,
    pub effects: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

impl Catalog {
    pub fn build(documents: Vec<Document>, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Self {
        let mut tables: BTreeMap<String, TableInfo> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut maintains = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut shapes: BTreeMap<String, ShapeDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut functions: BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut effects = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut effect_sites: BTreeMap<String, Site> = BTreeMap::new();
        for doc in &documents {
            for table in &doc.tables {
                if tables.contains_key(&table.name) {
                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&table.site, format!("duplicate table {}", table.name), "rename one of the two tables"));
                    continue;
                }
                let mut columns = BTreeMap::new();
                let mut ok = true;
                for column in &table.columns {
                    if column.name == "id" {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("table {} gets id implicitly; remove the id column", table.name), "delete this line"));
                        ok = false;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if columns.insert(column.name.clone(), column.type_.clone()).is_some() {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("duplicate column {}.{}", table.name, column.name), "rename one of the two columns"));
                        ok = false;
                    }
                }
                for field in &table.search {
                    if !matches!(columns.get(field), Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Text { .. } | TypeKind::Enum { .. }, .. })) {
                        diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&table.site, format!("search {} requires a text or enum column on {}", field, table.name), format!("declare `{field}: text <=2000` or `{field}: enum('one', 'two')` before `search {field}`")).guide("search"));
                    }
                }
                let mut indexes = vec![IndexDecl { name: "byId".into(), columns: vec!["id".into()], unique: true, refusal: None, words: None, computed: BTreeMap::new(), site: table.site.clone() }];
                for index in &table.indexes {
                    if indexes.iter().any(|existing| existing.name == index.name) {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&index.site, format!("duplicate index {}.{}", table.name, index.name), "name one ordering explicitly"));
                        ok = false;
                        continue;
                    }
                    for column in &index.columns {
                        if index.computed.contains_key(column) { continue; }
                        if column != "id" && !columns.contains_key(column) {
                            diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&index.site, format!("index {}.{} names unknown column {column}", table.name, index.name), name_rewrite(column, columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
                            ok = false;
                        }
                    }
                    for field in index.words.iter().flat_map(|(_, fields)| fields) {
                        match columns.get(field) {
                            Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Text { .. } | TypeKind::Enum { .. }, .. }) => {}
                            // The table stays in the catalog: one line is
                            // wrong, and its readers should hear about that
                            // line, not about a table that vanished.
                            Some(other) => diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&index.site, format!("index {}.{} tokenizes {field}, which is {}, not text or enum", table.name, index.name, crate::print::scalar_type(other)), format!("words(<a text or enum column of {}>)", table.name))),
                            None => diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&index.site, format!("index {}.{} tokenizes unknown column {field}", table.name, index.name), name_rewrite(field, columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None))),
                        }
                    }
                    for (name, expr) in &index.computed {
                        if let Err(error) = super::time_indexes::key(&table.name, &columns, name, expr) { diagnostics.push(error); }
                    }
                    indexes.push(index.clone());
                }
                let mut seen_effects = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
                for rule in &table.rules {
                    if !seen_effects.insert(rule.effect) {
                        if rule.effect == Effect::Read && table.rules.iter().any(|r| matches!(&r.value,Expr::Call{name,..} if matches!(name.as_str(), "$readThrough" | "$readThroughLive"))) {
                            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&rule.site,"E_READ_THROUGH",format!("{} declares another read rule beside read through",table.name),"keep read through as the whole read rule; put service grants on the parent").guide("rules"));
                        } else {
                            let first = table.rules.iter().find(|first| first.effect == rule.effect).unwrap();
                            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&rule.site, "E_QUARRY_DUPLICATE_RULE", format!("duplicate {} rule on {}; first declared at {}", rule.effect.word(), table.name, first.site), "keep one rule per effect; combine intended alternatives with or in that one rule").guide("rules"));
                        }
                        // Keep the usable declaration to avoid cascading unknown-table errors.

                    }
                }
                if ok {
                    let mut decl = table.clone();
                    let mut effects = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
                    decl.rules.retain(|rule| effects.insert(rule.effect));
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — rejected search metadata must not erase the usable table symbol.
                    // An invalid search field is a fault of that search line,
                    // not evidence that every later reference names an
                    // unknown table. Keep the usable table symbol and omit
                    // only search fields that could not be indexed.
                    decl.search.retain(|field| matches!(columns.get(field), Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Text { .. } | TypeKind::Enum { .. }, .. })));
                    // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — catalog consumers see the same
                    // implicit immutability as runtime schema consumers.
     1	//! Creation is a write even when an edit operation has the stronger guard.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#151-every-executed-write — conditional advice never infers creation policy.

# Rules

**Snapback 2 optimizes the edit-to-verified loop — the agent's building an app on it, and ours building it.**

Speed is the constraint. Everything below exists to keep the edit-to-verified loop short
and to stop this repository accreting apparatus faster than anyone can remove it. These
rules bind `snapback2/` and the LLPs numbered 1000 and up; Snapback 1 is baselined, not
exempt, and its numbers may only fall.

Each rule is **[check]** (enforced by `caps`) or **[review]** (enforced by a human).

## Budgets — every budget is a trade, not a limit

- **5 blocking checks, 60s total.** A sixth requires deleting one, in the same PR. **[check]**
- **59 documents in the working set** (`llp/current/`). Today's count. A 60th requires
  archiving one — which is just removing the link. Snapback 2 aims to leave this at 15;
  Snapback 1's links are archived at promotion. **[check]**
- **12 documents in the foundation** (`llp/foundation/`). **[check]**
- **This file: 700 words.** If it grows, something becomes a check or stops being a rule. **[check]**
- **1,500 lines per source file, baseline 204.** Generated files exempt — and generated
  files are built, not committed; so are benchmark run records under `benchmarks/runs/`,
  which are evidence, not source. The baseline is Snapback 1's oversize count on the day
  the rule landed; it may not grow, and `snapback2/` contributes zero to it. **[check]**

## Blocking checks

`caps` · `ref-check` · `snapback2/scripts/test.sh` · `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs --check` · `check-inventories`

## Loop shape

- **Every check reports all failures in one run.** Fail-fast is banned. **[check]**
- **Nothing blocks on anything slower than 60 seconds.** Slow verification runs on a
  schedule, attributed to the breaking commit, and files a bounded set of issues. **[review]**
- **Fix loops get 3 rounds.** Then stop: escalate to a human or descope. **[review]**

## Time budgets

Tracked every run of `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs` (appends `snapback2/metrics/loop.jsonl`).
A regression is a P0 with a name on it.

| | budget |
|---|---|
| Incremental rebuild after a one-line edit | 15s |
| Test what you just changed | 30s |
| Blocking gate suite | 60s |
| Full build, warm cache | 5 min |
| **`snapback2 check` on a program (the agent's product latency)** | **1s** |
| **`snapback2 doctor` boot-to-ready** | **0.5s** |

## Scope

- **`NOT-DOING.md` is binding.** Moving something onto the doing-list means writing why
  and taking something off. **[review]**
- **Delete; don't deprecate.** No compat shims or migration paths before promotion. **[review]**
- **Nothing is compiled, transpiled, or generated at runtime that could be built ahead of
  time.** **[review]**
- **A spec needs an implementer and a date, or it isn't written.** **[review]**
- **Only implemented specs bind.** A document with no running code behind it is a
  proposal, and a proposal cannot block a PR or justify a check. **[review]**

## Agents

- **Agents remove apparatus freely and add none.** An agent PR that adds a check, script,
  registry, config file, or design doc needs explicit human approval saying so. **[review]**
- **2026-09-06 session exception:** the user delegated the search implementation
  decisions; one `snapback2-core/build.rs` may generate pinned Unicode tables at
  build time, alongside the existing package build. No new gate, registry,
  runtime generator, or Unicode dependency is authorized by this exception. **[review]**
- **Snapback 2 LLPs are numbered 1000 and up**; `llp-create` must be told. **[review]**
- **Snapback 4 LLPs are numbered 3000 and up** (`snapback4/`, LLP 3000); it adopts these budgets
  and loop shape as its own and keeps its scope in `snapback4/NOT-DOING.md`. **[review]**

## Shared machines

- Warm, named lanes, one per stream of work. Never clone fresh; reuse the worktree.
- **Never `git stash`.** Use a temporary commit.
- Kill only PIDs you recorded at launch. Never by name or pattern.

                            format!("declare `{}: int` and bound the operation's argument or the stored column as {range}", param.name)).guide("types"));
                    }
                }
                functions.insert(function.name.clone(), function.clone());
            }

                if let Some(first) = effect_sites.get(&effect.name) {
                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&effect.site, format!("duplicate effect {}; first declared at {first}", effect.name), "rename one effect"));
                    continue;
                }
                effect_sites.insert(effect.name.clone(), effect.site.clone());
                effects.insert(effect.name.clone(), effect.module.clone());
            }
        }
        super::through::expand(&mut tables, diagnostics);
        for maintain in maintains.values_mut() { if let Some(source)=tables.get(&maintain.source) { super::maintains::inherited_keys(source,maintain); } }
        super::columns::resolve(&mut tables, diagnostics);

//! Independent security intent and conservative exposure obligations.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — a direct-rule theorem never clears an unmodeled path.
use super::{catalog::Catalog, Bound};
use crate::{ast, Diagnostic, Site};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Invariant {
    pub table: String,
    pub target: String,
    pub column: Option<String>,
    pub predicate: String,
    pub site: Site,
    pub policy: Option<RuleExpr>,
    pub operation: Option<String>,
    pub requirements: Vec<RuleExpr>,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub syntax: Option<ast::Expr>,
    pub unsupported: Option<String>,
}
impl Invariant {
    pub fn target_label(&self)->String {match &self.column{Some(c)=>format!("{}.{} {}",self.table,c,self.target),None=>format!("{}.{}",self.table,self.target)}}
}
pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Vec<Invariant> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for decl in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.invariants) {
        let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&decl.table) else {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET",format!("invariant names unknown table {}",decl.table),"declare the table or correct its name").guide("security")); continue;
        };
        let named_operation=operation::resolve(catalog,decl);
        if (!matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && named_operation.is_none())
            || decl.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"&&!info.columns.contains_key(c)) {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET","invariant needs a declared column or table effect","use invariant table.read only <policy>, table.column write only <policy>, or table always <predicate>").guide("security")); continue;
        }
        let effect=match decl.target.as_str(){"insert"=>ast::Effect::Insert,"update"|"write"=>ast::Effect::Update,"delete"=>ast::Effect::Delete,_=>ast::Effect::Read};
        // Independent intent may use authorized reference operands even when a
        // runtime read gate must use captured authority. It never installs a gate.
        let effect=if effect==ast::Effect::Read{ast::Effect::Insert}else{effect};
        let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect,bindings:Vec::new(),value:decl.value.clone(),site:decl.site.clone()};
        let (policy,unsupported)=match super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule) {
            Ok(rule)=>(Some(rule),None),Err(error)=>match temporal::bind(catalog,info,&decl.value) {
                Some(rule) if decl.target=="read" => (Some(rule),None),
                _ => (None,Some(format!("policy outside rule proof fragment: {} ({})",error.message,error.code))),
            },
        };
        let requirements=named_operation.as_ref().map(|(_,p)| operation::requirements(catalog,info,p)).unwrap_or_default();
        out.push(Invariant{operation:named_operation.map(|(name,_)|name),requirements,syntax:Some(decl.value.clone()),table:decl.table.clone(),target:decl.target.clone(),column:decl.column.clone(),predicate:crate::print::expression(&decl.value),site:decl.site.clone(),policy,unsupported});
    }
    out
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="lowercase")]
pub enum Status { Proved, Refuted, Unproved }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Verdict {
    pub invariant: Invariant,
    pub status: Status,
    pub direct_rule: Status,
    pub paths: Vec<String>,
    pub reasons: Vec<String>,
    pub counterexample: Option<challenges::Counterexample>,
    pub obligations: Vec<Obligation>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Report {
    pub format: u32,
    pub scope: String,
    pub exclusions: Vec<String>,
    pub invariants: Vec<Verdict>,
    pub exposures: Vec<Exposure>,
    pub roles: BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>,
    pub notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,

fn operation_name(module: &str, name: &str) -> String {
    if module == "schema" { name.to_owned() } else { format!("{module}.{name}") }
}
