//! Seeks and row-local scan predicates.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.000.001#4 — retain the binder's actual choice for why, never infer a new lookup or rewrite.
    pub(super) fn record_selection(&mut self, site: &str, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, result: &str, reason: &str) {
        let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
        let bindings = index.columns.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(position, column)| {
            let key = if named { read.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column)) } else { read.keys.get(position) }?;
            Some(format!("{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value)))
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        let origin = if self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())) { "synthesized; " } else { "" };
        let source = if origin.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("; read: {}", self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(read))) };
        let mut selection = format!("selected {} ({bindings}); result: {result}; {origin}{reason}{source}", index.name);
        if !self.variants.is_empty() {
            let when = self.variants.iter().map(|(name, present)| format!("{} {}", name.trim_start_matches("$intentPresent:").trim_start_matches("$intentSearch:"), if *present { "present" } else { "absent" })).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
            selection = format!("when {when}: {selection}");
        }
        self.selections.entry(site.into()).and_modify(|prior| { if !prior.contains(&selection) { prior.push_str("; "); prior.push_str(&selection); } }).or_insert(selection);
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — a covered total unique key reads one row; additional named equalities check that row.
    pub(super) fn read_value(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Expr> {
        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
        for key in &read.keys { if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::super::row_views::predicate_field(info, field, &read.site)?; } }
        if !read.seeks.is_empty() { return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek needs a bounded page", "t[prefix, col >= start] first 50 by index")); }
        let mut point = read.clone();
        if point.keys.is_empty() && info.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) {
            point.keys.push(ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), site: read.site.clone() }, site: read.site.clone() });
            point.index = Some("byGroup".into());
        } else if point.keys.is_empty() {
            return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{}[] reads nothing; a point read needs a key", read.table), format!("{}[id]", read.table)));
        }
        let implicit_group = mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && point.index.is_none();
        if implicit_group { point.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
        let (mut keys, index, mut selection) = self.select_index(info, &point, mode, out)?;
        super::super::point_key::check(info, &index, read, &keys.iter().map(|(column, _, ty)|
            (column.as_deref(), !ty.is_optional() && *ty != Ty::Unknown)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), false)?;
        if implicit_group { selection = "complete maintained group key".into(); }
        let extra = keys.len() > index.columns.len();
        let mut equalities = Vec::new();
        if extra {
            for (column, value, ty) in &mut keys {
                let column = column.as_ref().expect("named key");
                if column != "id" && !info.columns.contains_key(column) {
                    return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
                }
                let expected = self.key_ty(info, &index, column);
                if !ty.comparable(&expected) { return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
                *value = self.snapshot(value.clone(), out);
                if !index.columns.contains(column) { equalities.push((column.clone(), value.clone())); }
            }
            keys.retain(|(column, _, _)| index.columns.contains(column.as_ref().unwrap()));
        }
        let key = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
        let location = self.site();
        self.record_selection(&location, read, &index, if mode == ReadMode::Exists { "exists" } else if info.maintained.is_some() { "scalar" } else { "row or null" }, &selection);
        self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (read.site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
        let mut point = Read { site: location, table: read.table.clone(), index: index.name, key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
        if mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_none() {
            self.optional_lookup_note(read, &point);
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.4 — only a caller-proved existence read may use the whole owner partition.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && equalities.is_empty() {
            super::super::own_keys::existence(self.schema, &mut point);
        }
        if equalities.is_empty() { return Ok(if mode == ReadMode::Exists { Expr::Exists(point) } else { Expr::Get(point) }); }
        let row = self.temp("probe");
        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Get(point) });
        let mut condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied aggregate carries a null value, not a present group.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && info.maintained.is_some() {
            let present = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value")), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(present) };
        }
        for (column, value) in equalities {
            let test = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), &column)), right: Box::new(value) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(test) };
        }
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists { return Ok(condition); }
        out.push(Stmt::If { condition, then_body: Vec::new(), else_body: vec![Stmt::Assign { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) }] });
        Ok(ir::var(&row))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.3 — a seek constrains the first index column after the pinned prefix.
    pub(super) fn seek(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<ir::Range>> {
        if read.seeks.is_empty() { return Ok(None); }
        let mut range = ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None };
        for bound in &read.seeks {
            if index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) != Some(&bound.column) || read.matching.is_some() {
                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek compares the first unpinned column of a plain index", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
            }
            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column);
            let (value, ty) = self.field_value(&ObjectField { spread: false, name: bound.column.clone(), value: Some(bound.value.clone()), site: bound.site.clone() }, &expected, out)?;
            if !ty.comparable(&expected) || ty.is_optional() {
                let endpoint = print::expression(&bound.value);
                let rewrite = if ty.is_optional() {
                    format!("put the read inside `if {endpoint} != null:`, then `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
                } else {
                    format!("use a present {} endpoint in `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", expected.required().name(), read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
                };
                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, format!("seek on {}.{} expects present {}, got {}", read.table, bound.column, expected.required().name(), ty.name()), rewrite));
            }
            let slot = match bound.op.as_str() { ">" => &mut range.gt, ">=" => &mut range.gte, "<" => &mut range.lt, "<=" => &mut range.lte, _ => unreachable!("parser seek comparison") };
            if slot.is_some() { return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek bound is repeated", "write one lower and one upper bound")); }
            *slot = Some(Box::new(self.snapshot(value, out)));
        }
        if (range.gt.is_some() && range.gte.is_some()) || (range.lt.is_some() && range.lte.is_some()) {
            return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek has at most one lower and one upper bound", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
        }
        // Null sorts below every present value. An upper-only nullable seek
        // must skip the entire null segment, including its id suffixes.
        if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none()
            && read.seeks.first().is_some_and(|bound| self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column).is_optional()) {
            range.gt = Some(Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)));
        }
        Ok(Some(range))
    }
}

impl Binder<'_> {
    pub(super) fn variant(&self) -> Option<ir::VariantId> {
        (!self.variants.is_empty()).then(|| {
            let hash = snapback4_core::value::digest(&self.variants);
            std::array::from_fn(|i| u32::from_str_radix(&hash[i * 8..i * 8 + 8], 16).expect("hex digest"))
        })
    }

        let predicate = read.filter.as_ref()?;
        let (base, name, site) = trailing_shape(predicate)?;
        self.catalog.shapes.get(name)?;
        // Type the operand in isolation: no speculative setup, notes or source sites
        // escape. A valid row/object shape inside the predicate keeps its meaning.

I found two binding problems in the final code: the eligibility normalizer can treat a lookup through a named reference column as an ID lookup, and upload-gate deduplication can merge same-named mutations from different files. I’m checking their call paths and line references before giving the verdict.

//! Creation and transition risk questions, independent of authored invariants.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — these gates demand review, never infer a specification.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr, Stmt, WriteOp, ProgramKind, TypeKind};
use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
mod eligibility;
fn note(out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,repair:&str){out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,repair).guide("security"));}
fn walk(e:&Expr,mut f:impl FnMut(&Expr)){let mut e=e.clone();super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|f(e));}
fn uses(e:&Expr,arg:&str)->bool{let mut found=false;walk(e,|e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name==arg){found=true;});found}
fn conjunction(e:&Expr)->Vec<&Expr>{match e{Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"=>conjunction(left).into_iter().chain(conjunction(right)).collect(),_=>vec![e]}}
fn conditions(p:&ast::ProgramDecl)->Vec<&Expr>{p.body.iter().filter_map(|s|if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=s{Some(condition)}else{None}).collect()}
fn reads(e:&Expr)->BTreeSet<String>{let mut out=BTreeSet::new();walk(e,|e|match e{Expr::Read(r)|Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=>{out.insert(r.table.clone());},_=>{}});out}
fn nonnull(p:&ast::ProgramDecl,arg:&str)->bool{nonnull_mode(p,arg,false)}
fn nonnull_mode(p:&ast::ProgramDecl,arg:&str,branches:bool)->bool{p.body.iter().filter_map(|s|if let Stmt::Require{condition,code,..}=s&&(branches||code!="$advice"){Some(condition)}else{None}).flat_map(conjunction).any(|e|match e{
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="!="=>[(left.as_ref(),right.as_ref()),(right.as_ref(),left.as_ref())].iter().any(|(a,b)|matches!(a,Expr::Name{name,..} if name==arg)&&matches!(b,Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..})),_=>false,
})}
fn field_value<'a>(f:&'a ast::ObjectField)->Option<&'a Expr>{f.value.as_ref()}
struct Write<'a>{p:&'a ast::ProgramDecl,op:WriteOp,table:String,fields:Vec<ast::ObjectField>,keys:Vec<ast::KeyPart>,site:Site,context:ast::ProgramDecl,gate:RuleExpr,conditional:bool}
fn bound_role(bound:&Bound,catalog:&Catalog,info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,expr:&Expr,recipient:&str)->Option<RuleExpr>{
    let Expr::Exists{read,..}=expr else{return None;};
    // Bind a positive recipient probe after substituting only its principal.
    if !read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name==recipient)){return None;}
    let mut value=expr.clone();super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |e|if let Expr::Name{name,site}=e{
        if name=="viewer"{*e=Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str("service:security-assigner-marker".into()),site:site.clone()};}
        else if name==recipient{*name="viewer".into();}
    });
    let rule=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),site:expr.site().clone(),value}).ok()?;
    let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=&rule else{return None;};
    let principal=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,table)?;
    let pairs=super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    if !pairs.iter().any(|(c,v)|c==&principal&&*v==RuleTerm::Viewer){return None;}
    let roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
    let equalities=pairs.into_iter().filter(|(c,v)|roles.contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_)))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if equalities.is_empty(){return None;}
    let index=bound.schema.tables[table].indexes.iter().find(|(_,i)|i.unique&&i.columns()==vec![principal.clone()])?.0.clone();
    Some(RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index,key:vec![RuleTerm::Viewer],equalities,provenance:snapback4_core::ir::KeyProvenance::Computed})

}
pub(super) fn notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let docs=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|crate::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=Catalog::build(docs.clone(),&mut Vec::new());let mut writes=Vec::new();
    for p in docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
        for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){

            let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&table)else{continue;};

            writes.push(Write{p,op,table,fields,keys,site,context,conditional:if_absent||condition.is_some(),gate:RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([rule]).collect())});

    }

    let mut write_indexes=BTreeMap::<(String,String),BTreeSet<String>>::new();

                    }

                        note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{}: optional {} conditionally creates {} evidence beside a parent; is that evidence optional in the specification? A required child field does not require a child row.",w.p.name,arg.name,w.table),"keep intentional optional attachments optional; if parent evidence is mandatory, declare independent parent integrity intent and require the upload before parent creation");
                    }

                    }

                }

        }

                // that selected index avoids guessing from declared arity alone.

            }

        }
    }

}

pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){
    report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(write_advice::notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(creation_advice::notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(result_advice::notes(bound));
    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);for t in doc.tables{
        sites.insert(t.name.clone(),t.site.clone());for c in t.columns{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,c.name),c.site);}
        for r in t.rules{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,r.effect.word()),r.site);}
    }}
    for note in &mut report.notes{if note.site.line==0 {
        let key=note.message.split([' ',':']).next().unwrap_or("");
        note.site=sites.get(key).or_else(||sites.get(key.split('.').next().unwrap_or(""))).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Site::new(sources.first().map(|s|s.0.as_str()).unwrap_or("schema.q"),1,1));
    }}
}
//! Repairs lead; uncertainty stays visible without repeating every proof blocker.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#144-actions-precede-coverage-details — advice is separate from replayed failures.
use super::{Report, Status};

/// One consequence ordering shared by check and audit. Lower means more urgent.
pub fn note_priority(code: &str) -> u8 {
    match code {
        "N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT" | "N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE" => 0,
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" | "N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY" => 1,
        "N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE" | "N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" |
        "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_CALLER_TIME" | "N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE" |
        "N_CREATION_REASON" | "N_CREATION_MONEY" | "N_CONSTANT_APPROVAL" |
        "N_TRANSITION_BYPASS" | "N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK" | "N_DURATION_BOUNDS" |
        "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" | "N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY" | "N_EVIDENCE_REMOVAL" => 2,
        "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" | "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" => 5,
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" => 6,
        "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => 7,
        _ => 4,
    }
}

impl Report {
    /// Full structured evidence remains in the report; this is a human action list.
    pub fn action_text(&self, limit: usize) -> String {
        let failures = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v| v.status == Status::Refuted &&
            v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| c.reachability.is_reachable())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut notes = self.notes.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        notes.sort_by_key(|n| (note_priority(&n.code), n.site.file.clone(), n.site.line, n.site.col, &n.code, &n.message));
        notes.dedup_by(|a,b| a.site == b.site && a.code == b.code && a.message == b.message);
        let mut out = format!("security actions: {} fix{}, {} review{}\n", failures.len(),
            if failures.len() == 1 { "" } else { "es" }, notes.len(), if notes.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" });
        for v in &failures {
            let c = v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
            out.push_str(&format!("  FIX {}: {} via {} as {} ({}); tighten enforcement to the declared intent, then run snapback4 test\n",
                v.invariant.site, v.invariant.target_label(), c.operation, c.viewer, serde_json::to_value(&c.reachability).unwrap().as_str().unwrap()));
        }
        let mut shown = 0;
        for note in notes.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(i,n)|
            (i < limit || note_priority(&n.code) <= 2).then_some(*n)) {
            shown += 1;
            out.push_str(&format!("  REVIEW {} {}: {}\n    {}\n",note.site,note.code,note.message,note.rewrite));
        }
        if shown < notes.len() { out.push_str(&format!("  {} more reviews: snapback4 audit --all or check --verbose\n", notes.len()-shown)); }
        let proved = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v| v.status == Status::Proved).count();
        let refuted = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==Status::Refuted).count();
        let unproved = self.invariants.len()-proved-refuted;
        out.push_str(&format!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted, {unproved} unproved ({} failing)\n", failures.len()));
        let supplemental = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v| v.status == Status::Refuted &&
            v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| !c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
        if supplemental > 0 {out.push_str(&format!("  {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations are non-failing\n"));}
        if unproved > 0 || !self.campaign_coverage.is_empty() {
            out.push_str(&format!("  next: snapback4 test; {} coverage gaps (audit --all/JSON keeps every reason)\n",self.campaign_coverage.len()));
        }
        if self.invariants.is_empty() {out.push_str("  next: answer snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft from the spec; declare intent and run snapback4 test\n");}
        out
    }
}

codex
**NOT READY** at `dd2db9a14bc0`. Two material binding issues remain:

1. **[P2] A non-ID lookup can suppress the wrong applicant’s eligibility question.** [eligibility.rs:14](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b22-sec7/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/creation_advice/eligibility.rs:14) treats any single non-`id` key containing a typed reference argument as that row’s ID. With `unique related` on `apps.related: apps ?`, reading `other = apps[related = application]`, checking `flags[other.owner]`, then approving `apps[id = application]` incorrectly matches the recheck to the approved applicant. `N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK` is suppressed despite checking another row. Explicit named non-ID selectors must retain their distinct identity; the `.id` path has the same fallback.

2. **[P2] Upload gates collide across modules.** [creation_advice.rs:72](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b22-sec7/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/creation_advice.rs:72) deduplicates by bare mutation name and argument name. Distinct `a.create(photo)` and `b.create(photo)` operations, each satisfying the mandatory-media detector, produce only the first upload gate—even when they create different tables. The second obligation has no individual review ID. Include source/module identity in deduplication and preserve distinct sites during final diagnostic deduplication.

These are static counterexamples; neither is covered by the focused regressions. No builds or tests ran, no files changed, and no credentials or prior review outputs were read.
